│ LLM         │ 💬 Now I understand the issue. Looking at the code, I can see th
│ Response    │
│             │ 1. `intermediate_cache1` is created with shape `(M, top_k_num, N
│             │ CHUNK_SIZE)`
│             │ 2. `intermediate_cache2` is created with shape `(M * top_k_num,
│             │ 3. `intermediate_cache3` is created with shape `(M, top_k_num, w
│             │
│             │ The issue is that when `tokens_in_chunk < CHUNK_SIZE` (which hap
│             │ slices the intermediate caches:
│             │ ```python
│             │ intermediate_cache1 = intermediate_cache1[:tokens_in_chunk]
│             │ intermediate_cache2 = intermediate_cache2[:tokens_in_chunk * top
│             │ intermediate_cache3 = intermediate_cache3[:tokens_in_chunk]
│             │ ```
│             │
│             │ However, looking at the shapes more carefully:
│             │ - `intermediate_cache2` has shape `(M * top_k_num, N // 2)` but
│             │ `(tokens_in_chunk * top_k_num, N // 2)`
│             │ - But `intermediate_cache3` has shape `(M, top_k_num, w2.shape[1
